generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    //   // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n        Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char**      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n    \\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n    int count = 0;\\n    \\n    while (count < N) {\\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n        \\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const // size gx*gy+1\n    std::vector<int> cell_pts;   // point indices sorted by cell\n\n    void build(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold) {\n        const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n        double max_x = 0.0, max_y = 0.0;\n        min_x = min_y = 0.0;\n        if (N > 0) {\n            min_x = max_x = points[0].x;\n            min_y = max_y = points[0].y;\n        }\n        for (int i = 1; i < N; ++i) {\n            min_x = std::min(min_x, points[i].x); max_x = std::max(max_x, points[i].x);\n            min_y = std::min(min_y, points[i].y); max_y = std::max(max_y, points[i].y);\n        }\n        const double extent = std::max(max_x - min_x, max_y - min_y);\n        const int max_dim = std::max(1, std::min(2048, static_cast<int>(2.0 * std::sqrt(static_cast<double>(N))) + 1));\n        cell = std::max(threshold, extent / max_dim);\n        if (!(cell > 0.0)) cell = 1.0;\n        gx = std::min(max_dim, static_cast<int>((max_x - min_x) / cell) + 1);\n        gy = std::min(max_dim, static_cast<int>((max_y - min_y) / cell) + 1);\n\n        std::vector<int> cell_of(N);\n        cell_start.assign(static_cast<size_t>(gx) * gy + 1, 0);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            int cx, cy;\n            cellCoords(points[i], cx, cy);\n            cell_of[i] = cy * gx + cx;\n            cell_start[cell_of[i] + 1]++;\n        }\n        for (size_t c = 1; c < cell_start.size(); ++c) cell_start[c] += cell_start[c - 1];\n        cell_pts.resize(N);\n        std::vector<int> fill(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end() - 1);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) cell_pts[fill[cell_of[i]]++] = i;\n    }\n\n    inline void cellCoords(const Point& p, int& cx, int& cy) const {\n        cx = std::clamp(static_cast<int>((p.x - min_x) / cell), 0, gx - 1);\n        cy = std::clamp(static_cast<int>((p.y - min_y) / cell), 0, gy - 1);\n    }\n\n    // Call f(q) for every point q in the 3x3 neighbourhood of p's cell\n    template <typename F>\n    inline void forNeighbourhood(const Point& p, F&& f) const {\n        int cx, cy;\n        cellCoords(p, cx, cy);\n        for (int y = std::max(0, cy - 1); y <= std::min(gy - 1, cy + 1); ++y) {\n            const int row = y * gx;\n            const int x0 = std::max(0, cx - 1), x1 = std::min(gx - 1, cx + 1);\n            for (int k = cell_start[row + x0]; k < cell_start[row + x1 + 1]; ++k) {\n                f(cell_pts[k]);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers for candidate cluster generation\nstruct CandidateScratch {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> x, y, max_dist;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster.\n//\n// Equivalent to iteratively adding the unclustered point whose maximum distance\n// to all current members is smallest (ties -> smallest point index), as long as\n// that maximum distance is < threshold. The maximum distance of each candidate\n// is maintained incrementally, and candidates are restricted to points within\n// threshold of the seed (all others can never be added).\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             CandidateScratch& s,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n\n    int size = 1;\n    int m = static_cast<int>(s.idx.size());\n    int* __restrict ci = s.idx.data();\n    double* __restrict cx = s.x.data();\n    double* __restrict cy = s.y.data();\n    double* __restrict cd = s.max_dist.data();\n\n    while (m > 0) {\n        // Select candidate with minimal max distance (ties -> smallest index)\n        int best = 0;\n        double best_d = cd[0];\n        int best_i = ci[0];\n        for (int j = 1; j < m; ++j) {\n            const double d = cd[j];\n            if (d < best_d || (d == best_d && ci[j] < best_i)) {\n                best = j; best_d = d; best_i = ci[j];\n            }\n        }\n        ++size;\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(best_i);\n        const Point np = {cx[best], cy[best]};\n\n        // Update max distances with the new member and drop candidates that\n        // can no longer be added (max distance only grows).\n        int w = 0;\n        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {\n            if (j == best) continue;\n            const double dx = cx[j] - np.x;\n            const double dy = cy[j] - np.y;\n            const double nd = std::max(cd[j], std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));\n            if (nd < threshold) {\n                ci[w] = ci[j]; cx[w] = cx[j]; cy[w] = cy[j]; cd[w] = nd;\n                ++w;\n            }\n        }\n        m = const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    Grid grid;\n    grid.build(points, threshold);\n\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> cardinality(N, 0);\n\n    // Candidate cardinalities of a seed only depend on the clustered state of\n    // points within threshold of the seed; they are cached and recomputed only\n    // when such a point becomes clustered.\n    std::vector<int> work(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) work[i] = i;\n\n    // Ordered by (largest cardinality, smallest seed index)\n    auto key = [](int card, int seed) { return (static_cast<long long>(-card) << 32) | static_cast<unsigned>(seed); };\n    std::vector<long long> order;\n    std::set<long long> ranking;\n\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> thread_dirty(omp_get_max_threads());\n    CandidateScratch main_scratch;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // (Re)compute candidate cluster cardinalities of dirty seeds\n        const int W = static_cast<int>(work.size());\n        #pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            CandidateScratch scratch;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int k = 0; k < W; ++k) {\n                const int seed = work[k];\n                cardinality[seed] = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, grid,\n                                                             threshold, scratch);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int k = 0; k < W; ++k) {\n            const int seed = work[k];\n            ranking.insert(key(cardinality[seed], seed));\n            dirty[seed] = 0;\n        }\n\n        // Best seed: maximum cardinality, first (smallest index) on ties\n        const int best_seed = static_cast<int>(static_cast<unsigned>(*ranking.begin() & 0xffffffffLL));\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                 main_scratch, &cluster.members);\n        const int M = static_cast<int>(cluster.members.size());\n\n        for (int i = 0; i < M; ++i) {\n            const int p = cluster.members[i];\n            clustered[p] = 1;\n            ranking.erase(key(cardinality[p], p));\n        }\n        remaining -= M;\n\n        // Mark unclustered seeds within threshold of new members as dirty\n        #pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            std::vector<int>& local = thread_dirty[omp_get_thread_num()];\n            local.clear();\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 4)\n            for (int i = 0; i < M; ++i) {\n // size gx*gy+1\n    std::vector<int> cell_pts;   // point indices sorted by cell\n\n    void build(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold) {\n        const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n        double max_x = 0.0, max_y = 0.0;\n        min_x = min_y = 0.0;\n        if (N > 0) {\n            min_x = max_x = points[0].x;\n            min_y = max_y = points[0].y;\n        }\n        for (int i = 1; i < N; ++i) {\n            min_x = std::min(min_x, points[i].x); max_x = std::max(max_x, points[i].x);\n            min_y = std::min(min_y, points[i].y); max_y = std::max(max_y, points[i].y);\n        }\n        const double extent = std::max(max_x - min_x, max_y - min_y);\n        const int max_dim = std::max(1, std::min(2048, static_cast<int>(2.0 * std::sqrt(static_cast<double>(N))) + 1));\n        cell = std::max(threshold, extent / max_dim);\n        if (!(cell > 0.0)) cell = 1.0;\n        gx = std::min(max_dim, static_cast<int>((max_x - min_x) / cell) + 1);\n        gy = std::min(max_dim, static_cast<int>((max_y - min_y) / cell) + 1);\n\n        std::vector<int> cell_of(N);\n        cell_start.assign(static_cast<size_t>(gx) * gy + 1, 0);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            int cx, cy;\n            cellCoords(points[i], cx, cy);\n            cell_of[i] = cy * gx + cx;\n            cell_start[cell_of[i] + 1]++;\n        }\n        for (size_t c = 1; c < cell_start.size(); ++c) cell_start[c] += cell_start[c - 1];\n        cell_pts.resize(N);\n        std::vector<int> fill(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end() - 1);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) cell_pts[fill[cell_of[i]]++] = i;\n    }\n\n    inline void cellCoords(const Point& p, int& cx, int& cy) const {\n        cx = std::clamp(static_cast<int>((p.x - min_x) / cell), 0, gx - 1);\n        cy = std::clamp(static_cast<int>((p.y - min_y) / cell), 0, gy - 1);\n    }\n\n    // Call f(q) for every point q in the 3x3 neighbourhood of p's cell\n    template <typename F>\n    inline void forNeighbourhood(const Point& p, F&& f) const {\n        int cx, cy;\n        cellCoords(p, cx, cy);\n        for (int y = std::max(0, cy - 1); y <= std::min(gy - 1, cy + 1); ++y) {\n            const int row = y * gx;\n            const int x0 = std::max(0, cx - 1), x1 = std::min(gx - 1, cx + 1);\n            for (int k = cell_start[row + x0]; k < cell_start[row + x1 + 1]; ++k) {\n                f(cell_pts[k]);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers for candidate cluster generation\nstruct CandidateScratch {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> x, y, max_dist;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster.\n//\n// Equivalent to iteratively adding the unclustered point whose maximum distance\n// to all current members is smallest (ties -> smallest point index), as long as\n// that maximum distance is < threshold. The maximum distance of each candidate\n// is maintained incrementally, and candidates are restricted to points within\n// threshold of the seed (all others can never be added).\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             CandidateScratch& s,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    s.idx.clear(); s.x.clear(); s.y.clear(); s.max_dist.clear();\n    const Point& sp = points[seed_point];\n    grid.forNeighbourhood(sp, [&](int q) {\n        if (q == seed_point || clustered[q]) return;\n        const double d = distance(points[q], sp);\n        if (d < threshold) {\n            s.idx.push_back(q);\n            s.x.push_back(points[q].x);\n            s.y.push_back(points[q].y);\n            s.max_dist.push_back(d);\n        }\n    });\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n\n    int size = 1;\n    int m = static_cast<int>(s.idx.size());\n    int* __restrict ci = s.idx.data();\n    double* __restrict cx = s.x.data();\n    double* __restrict cy = s.y.data();\n    double* __restrict cd = s.max_dist.data();\n\n    while (m > 0) {\n        // Select candidate with minimal max distance (ties -> smallest index)\n        int best = 0;\n        double best_d = cd[0];\n        int best_i = ci[0];\n        for (int j = 1; j < m; ++j) {\n            const double d = cd[j];\n            if (d < best_d || (d == best_d && ci[j] < best_i)) {\n                best = j; best_d = d; best_i = ci[j];\n            }\n        }\n        ++size;\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(best_i);\n        const Point np = {cx[best], cy[best]};\n\n        // Update max distances with the new member and drop candidates that\n        // can no longer be added (max distance only grows).\n        int w = 0;\n        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {\n            if (j == best) continue;\n            const double dx = cx[j] - np.x;\n            const double dy = cy[j] - np.y;\n            const double nd = std::max(cd[j], std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));\n            if (nd < threshold) {\n                ci[w] = ci[j]; cx[w] = cx[j]; cy[w] = cy[j]; cd[w] = nd;\n                ++w;\n            }\n        }\n        m = w;\n    }\n    return size;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    Grid grid;\n    grid.build(points, threshold);\n\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> cardinality(N, 0);\n\n    // Candidate cardinalities of a seed only depend on the clustered state of\n    // points within threshold of the seed; they are cached and recomputed only\n    // when such a point becomes clustered.\n    std::vector<int> work(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) work[i] = i;\n\n    // Ordered by (largest cardinality, smallest seed index)\n    auto key = [](int card, int seed) { return (static_cast<long long>(-card) << 32) | static_cast<unsigned>(seed); };\n    std::vector<long long> order;\n    std::set<long long> ranking;\n\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> thread_dirty(omp_get_max_threads());\n    CandidateScratch main_scratch;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // (Re)compute candidate cluster cardinalities of dirty seeds\n        const int W = static_cast<int>(work.size());\n        #pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            CandidateScratch scratch;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int k = 0; k < W; ++k) {\n                const int seed = work[k];\n                cardinality[seed] = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, grid,\n                                                             threshold, scratch);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int k = 0; k < W; ++k) {\n            const int seed = work[k];\n            ranking.insert(key(cardinality[seed], seed));\n            dirty[seed] = 0;\n        }\n\n        // Best seed: maximum cardinality, first (smallest index) on ties\n        const int best_seed = static_cast<int>(static_cast<unsigned>(*ranking.begin() & 0xffffffffLL));\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                 main_scratch, &cluster.members);\n        const int M = static_cast<int>(cluster.members.size());\n\n        for (int i = 0; i < M; ++i) {\n            const int p = cluster.members[i];\n            clustered[p] = 1;\n            ranking.erase(key(cardinality[p], p));\n        }\n        remaining -= M;\n\n        // Mark unclustered seeds within threshold of new members as dirty\n        #pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            std::vector<int>& local = thread_dirty[omp_get_thread_num()];\n            local.clear();\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 4)\n            for (int i = 0; i < M; ++i) {\n                const Point& pp = points[cluster.members[i]];\n                grid.forNeighbourhood(pp, [&](int q) {\n                    if (clustered[q]) return;\n                    if (distance(points[q], pp) < threshold) {\n                        char old;\n                        #pragma omp atomic capture\n                        { old = dirty[q]; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfgwfEqpBpGh3gZDc1oXr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The clustering in `qtclustering/` is now parallelized with OpenMP and gives exactly the same output as the original. On 2,000 points the original takes 88 s. The new version takes 22.4 s on 1 thread and 0.36 s on 128 threads for 20,000 points, and handles 100,000 points in 18 s.\n\n**Changes to `qtclustering.cpp`.** Only the clustering code changed; data generation, validation and output are untouched.\n- **Spatial grid:** points are binned into cells at least as wide as the threshold. A seed's possible cluster members are then only the points within threshold of it in the 3×3 neighbouring cells, instead of all N points.\n- **Faster cluster building:** each candidate keeps a running maximum distance to the cluster, updated only against the newly added point. This removes the rescan of all members on every step. Candidates that reach the threshold are dropped for good, and finding the next point to add happens in the same pass.\n- **Cached results:** a seed's candidate cluster size only changes when a point within threshold of it gets clustered. Sizes are cached and recomputed only for those seeds, and the best seed comes from an ordered set instead of a full rescan.\n- **One persistent parallel region:** a single `#pragma omp parallel` wraps the whole loop. The seed computations and the marking of affected seeds use dynamic `omp for`, and the short serial updates run in `omp single`. Per-iteration regions were noticeably slower at high thread counts on this busy machine.\n- **Same results:** ties are still broken by smallest point index, and clusters and their member order are identical to the original.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. The executable is still `qtclustering`, no files were added, and there are no new dependencies.\n\n**Checks:**\n- **Exact match:** for `-n 1000`, `-n 2000`, `-n 1500 -t 1.0`, `-n 1200 -t 3.5` and `-n 500 -t 0.3`, output from the original build (minus timing lines) is identical to the new one at 1, 7, 64 and 256 threads, including the membership hash and validation.\n- **Larger inputs:** at 20,000 points the hash is the same at 1 and 128 threads, and validation passes. At 100,000 points validation passes.\n\n**Timing for 20,000 points:**\n\n| Threads | Time |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 22.4 s |\n| 64 | 0.52 s |\n| 128 | 0.36 s |\n| 256 | 0.40–0.50 s |\n\nThe machine is 2× 64-core EPYC 7763 with 2 hardware threads per core, and it was shared, with load averages up to about 157. Timings varied a lot between runs. The new version takes 22.4 s on 1 thread and 0.36 s on 128 threads for 20,000 points, and handles 100,000 points in 18 s.\n\n**Changes to `qtclustering.cpp`.** Only the clustering code changed; data generation, validation and output are untouched.\n- **Spatial grid:** points are binned into cells at least as wide as the threshold. A seed's possible cluster members are then only the points within threshold of it in the 3×3 neighbouring cells, instead of all N points.\n- **Faster cluster building:** each candidate keeps a running maximum distance to the cluster, updated only against the newly added point. This removes the rescan of all members on every step. Candidates that reach the threshold are dropped for good, and finding the next point to add happens in the same pass.\n- **Cached results:** a seed's candidate cluster size only changes when a point within threshold of it gets clustered. Sizes are cached and recomputed only for those seeds, and the best seed comes from an ordered set instead of a full rescan.\n- **One persistent parallel region:** a single `#pragma omp parallel` wraps the whole loop. The seed computations and the marking of affected seeds use dynamic `omp for`, and the short serial updates run in `omp single`. Per-iteration regions were noticeably slower at high thread counts on this busy machine.\n- **Same results:** ties are still broken by smallest point index, and clusters and their member order are identical to the original.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.
